!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-explanation undrr-definition-of-disaster "The globally accepted definition of a disaster provided by the United Nations Office for Disaster Risk Reduction (UNDRR), which characterizes a disaster as a serious disruption of a community or society's functioning due to hazardous events interacting with conditions of exposure, vulnerability, and capacity, leading to various losses and impacts."))
!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (related-to undrr-definition-of-disaster hazard))

!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-assertion undrr-definition-of-disaster assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-0))
!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-text assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-0 "a serious disruption of the functioning of a community or a society at any scale due to hazardous events interacting with conditions of exposure, vulnerability and capacity, leading to one or more of the following: human, material, economic and environmental losses and impacts."))
!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-type assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-0 fact))
!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-source assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-0 "https://www.UNDRR.org/terminology/disaster"))

!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-text assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-1 "this definition shifts the focus away from the hazard event alone and toward the societal conditions that allow such an event to cause widespread harm."))
!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-type assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-1 fact))
!(add-atom &disaster-preparedness-response-and-recovery-space (has-citation assertion-undrr-definition-of-disaster-1 "1"))
